=== FRESH CLONE UNIT TEST SUITE EXECUTION & REGRESSION CLASSIFICATION ===
Audit Date: 2026-10-29
Command: node_modules/.bin/tsx --test --test-concurrency=1 tests/unit/*.test.ts
Concurrency: 1 (Sequential execution, no timeout kill)

================================================================================
SECTION 1: EXPLANATION OF CANCELLED TESTS (143 in raw run / 102 in baseline & fixed)
================================================================================
Node.js native test runner (node:test) marks subtests as: failureType: 'cancelledByParent'
whenever a parent suite's before() hook fails or throws an exception.

Root Cause Analysis:
1. In tests/unit, 25 test suites interact with the test database and include a security guard:
   e.g., if (!process.env.DATABASE_URL) throw new Error('DATABASE_URL is not set...')
2. In the raw run (without .env.test loaded into shell), the before() hooks in these suites failed closed,
   causing node:test to cancel all child tests (143 cancelled tests total across 25 suites).
3. When the same command was run against baseline 54bf0a2 and tip with identical .env.test, EXACTLY 102 tests
   were cancelled in BOTH runs because those 25 test suites rely on process.env.DATABASE_URL from the parent shell
   rather than internal dotenv calls. Zero test cancellations were introduced by the B-001 backport.
